Theorem 221.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let the numerical_analysis structure S satisfy the conditions of convergence analysis framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 624. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 3 and converges at rate O(1/n^3).

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the convergence analysis condition on numerical_analysis structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 624. The convergence follows from the convergence analysis estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 3. QED.

Theorem 221.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Every structural decomposition deformation of the numerical_analysis object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 185. The moduli space has dimension at most 433 with 2 connected components.

Proof: By the structural decomposition principle applied to numerical_analysis theory, we decompose the problem into 185 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 433. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(2/n). QED.

Theorem 221.3 (Optimality Condition)

Statement: The numerical_analysis operator L associated with optimality condition theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 358. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 903 with 5 eigenvalues on the boundary.
